# Active Master Directions by Regulated Entity

This index organizes all Master Directions issued by the Reserve Bank of India into primary regulated entity domains. Master Directions represent **Tier 1 Controlling Law** and supersede legacy circulars.

## Table of Contents

- [All-India Financial Institutions & Miscellaneous](#all-india-financial-institutions-miscellaneous) (50 directions)
- [Commercial Banks (SCBs, SFBs, PBs, RRBs)](#commercial-banks-scbs-sfbs-pbs-rrbs) (148 directions)
- [Cross-Sectoral, Governance & Consumer Protection](#cross-sectoral-governance-consumer-protection) (30 directions)
- [Financial Markets, Money Market & Derivatives](#financial-markets-money-market-derivatives) (26 directions)
- [Foreign Exchange Management (FEMA) & Cross-Border](#foreign-exchange-management-fema-cross-border) (15 directions)
- [Housing Finance Companies (HFCs)](#housing-finance-companies-hfcs) (1 directions)
- [Non-Banking Financial Companies (NBFCs & ARCs)](#non-banking-financial-companies-nbfcs-arcs) (50 directions)
- [Payment System Operators (PSOs) & Authorized Payment Entities](#payment-system-operators-psos-authorized-payment-entities) (11 directions)
- [Primary (Urban) Co-operative Banks (UCBs)](#primary-urban-co-operative-banks-ucbs) (66 directions)
